          maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);\n   197\t            }\n   198\t        }\n   199\t\n   200\t        // Collect all indices\n   201\t        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());\n   202\t        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;\n   203\t\n   204\t        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);\n   205\t    }\n   206\t\n   207\tprivate:\n   208\t    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {\n   209\t        minBound = nodeMin;\n   210\t        maxBound = nodeMax;\n   211\t        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n   212\t        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n   213\t\n   214\t        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n   215\t        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n   216\t            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n   217\t            triangleIndices = indices;\n   218\t            return;\n   219\t        }\n   220\t\n   221\t        // Subdivide into 8 children\n   222\t        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\n   223\t        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\n   224\t\n   225\t        for (size_t idx : indices) {\n   226\t            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\n   227\t\n   228\t            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\n   229\t            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n   230\t                Vec3 childCenter = center;\n   231\t                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n   232\t                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n   233\t                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n   234\t\n   235\t                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n   236\t                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n   237\t                }\n   238\t            }\n   239\t        }\n   240\t\n   241\t        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n   242\t        bool canSplit = false;\n   243\t        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n   244\t            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n   245\t                canSplit = true;\n   246\t                break;\n   247\t            }\n   248\t        }\n   249\t\n   250\t        if (!canSplit) {\n   251\t            triangleIndices = indices;\n   252\t            return;\n   253\t        }\n   254\t\n   255\t        // Build children\n   256\t        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n   257\t            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {\n   258\t                Vec3 childMin = center;\n   259\t                Vec3 childMax = center;\n   260\t                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n   261\t                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n   262\t                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n   263\t                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n   264\t                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n   265\t                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;\n   266\t\n   267\t                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\n   268\t                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\n   269\t                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);\n   270\t            }\n   271\t        }\n   272\t    }\n   273\t\n   274\tpublic:\n   275\t    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n   276\t    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n   277\t        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n   278\t        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n   279\t        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n   280\t\n   281\t        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n   282\t        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n   283\t        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n   284\t\n   285\t        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n   286\t        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n   287\t        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n   288\t\n   289\t        return true;\n   290\t    }\n   291\t\n   292\t    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n   293\t    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n   294\t    template<typename Func>\n   295\t    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n   296\t        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n   297\t        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n   298\t            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n   299\t                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n   300\t            }\n   301\t            return false;\n   302\t        }\n   303\t\n   304\t        // Otherwise, descend to children\n   305\t        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n   306\t            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n   307\t                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n   308\t            }\n   309\t        }\n   310\t        return false;\n   311\t    }\n   312\t};\n   313\t\n   314\t// ============================================================================\n   315\t// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n   316\t// ============================================================================\n   317\t\n   318\t// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\n   319\tclass IcosphereMesh {\n   320\tpublic:\n   321\t    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n   322\t\n   323\t    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n   324\t        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n   325\t        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n   326\t\n   327\t        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n   328\t            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n   329\t            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n   330\t            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n   331\t            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n   332\t            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n   333\t            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n   334\t            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n   335\t            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n   336\t            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n   337\t            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n   338\t            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n   339\t            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n   340\t        };\n   341\t\n   342\t        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n   343\t        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n   344\t            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n   345\t            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n   346\t            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n   347\t            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n   348\t        };\n   349\t\n   350\t        // Subdivide\n   351\t        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n   352\t            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n   353\t            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n   354\t\n   355\t            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n   356\t                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n   357\t                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n   358\t                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n   359\t\n   360\t                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n   361\t                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n   362\t                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n   363\t                vertices.push_back(mid);\n   364\t                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n   365\t                return idx;\n   366\t            };\n   367\t\n   368\t            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n   369\t                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n   370\t                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n   371\t                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n   372\t\n   373\t                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n   374\t                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n   375\t                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n   376\t                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n   377\t            }\n   378\t            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n   379\t        }\n   380\t\n   381\t        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n   382\t        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n   383\t        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n   384\t            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n   385\t            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n   386\t        }\n   387\t    }\n   388\t};\n   389\t\n   390\t// ============================================================================\n   391\t// Random Number Generation\n   392\t// ============================================================================\n   393\t\n   394\tclass RandomGenerator {\n   395\t    std::mt19937 rng;\n   396\t    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\n   397\tpublic:\n   398\t    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n   399\t    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n   400\t};\n   401\t\n   402\t// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\n   403\tVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n   404\t    val_t u = rng.rand();\n   405\t    val_t v = rng.rand();\n   406\t    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n   407\t        u = 1.0f - u;\n   408\t        v = 1.0f - v;\n   409\t    }\n   410\t    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n   411\t    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n   412\t    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n   413\t}\n   414\t\n   415\t// ============================================================================\n   416\t// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n   417\t// ============================================================================\n   418\t\n   419\tval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n   420\t                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n   421\t    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n   422\t    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n   423\t    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n   424\t    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n   425\t\n   426\t    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n   427\t\n   428\t    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n   429\t    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n   430\t    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n   431\t    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n   432\t\n   433\t    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n   434\t    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n   435\t    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n   436\t\n   437\t    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n   438\t}\n   439\t\n   440\t// ============================================================================\n   441\t// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n   442\t// ============================================================================\n   443\t\n   444\t// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n   445\t// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\n   446\tbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n   447\t                  const Octree& octree,\n   448\t                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n   449\t    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n   450\t    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n   451\t    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n   452\t    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n   453\t\n   454\t    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n   455\t        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n   456\t\n   457\t        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n   458\t        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n   459\t            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n   460\t        }\n   461\t        return false;\n   462\t    });\n   463\t}\n   464\t\n   465\t// ============================================================================\n   466\t// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n   467\t// ============================================================================\n   468\t\n   469\t// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\n   470\tval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n   471\t    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n   472\t    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n   473\t    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n   474\t}\n   475\t\n   476\t// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\n   477\tval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n   478\t                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n   479\t                  const Octree& octree,\n   480\t                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n   481\t    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n   482\t    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n   483\t\n   484\t    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n   485\t    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n   486\t\n   487\t    val_t kij = ZERO;\n   488\t\n   489\t    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n   490\t        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n   491\t        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n   492\t\n   493\t        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n   494\t\n   495\t        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n   496\t        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n   497\t        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n   498\t\n   499\t        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n   500\t        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n   501\t\n   502\t        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n   503\t\n   504\t        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n   505\t    }\n   506\t\n   507\t    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n   508\t}\n   509\t\n   510\t// ============================================================================\n   511\t// Tau (time delay) Computation\n   512\t// ============================================================================\n   513\t\n   514\tint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n   515\t    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n   516\t    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n   517\t}\n   518\t\n   519\t// ============================================================================\n   520\t// Room Response Simulation State\n   521\t// ============================================================================\n   522\t\n   523\tstruct SimulationState {\n   524\t    size_t numTriangles;\n   525\t    size_t numTimesteps;\n   526\t\n   527\t    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n   528\t    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n   529\t    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n   530\t    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n   531\t    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n   532\t    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n   533\t    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n   534\t    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n   535\t\n   536\t    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n   537\t\n   538\t    size_t sourceIndex;\n   539\t\n   540\t    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n   541\t    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n   542\t};\n   543\t\n   544\t// ============================================================================\n   545\t// Initialization\n   546\t// ============================================================================\n   547\t\n   548\tvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n   549\t                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n   550\t    // Generate mesh\n   551\t    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n   552\t    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n   553\t    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n   554\t    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n   555\t    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n   556\t\n   557\t    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n   558\t\n   559\t    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n   560\t    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n   561\t    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n   562\t\n   563\t    // Initialize areas\n   564\t    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n   565\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n   566\t        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n   567\t    }\n   568\t\n   569\t    // Initialize reflectivity\n   570\t    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n   571\t\n   572\t    // Initialize matrices\n   573\t    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n   574\t    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n   575\t    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n   576\t    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n   577\t    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n   578\t\n   579\t    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n   580\t    size_t timeOn = 0;\n   581\t    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n   582\t    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n   583\t        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n   584\t    }\n   585\t}\n   586\t\n   587\t// ============================================================================\n   588\t// Precomputation Phase\n   589\t// ============================================================================\n   590\t\n   591\tvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n   592\t    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n   593\t    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n   594\t\n   595\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n   596\t        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n   597\t            if (i == j) continue;\n   598\t            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n   599\t                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n   600\t        }\n   601\t        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n   602\t            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n   603\t        }\n   604\t    }\n   605\t}\n   606\t\n   607\tvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n   608\t    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n   609\t\n   610\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n   611\t        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n   612\t            if (i == j) continue;\n   613\t            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n   614\t                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n   615\t        }\n   616\t    }\n   617\t}\n   618\t\n   619\t// ============================================================================\n   620\t// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n   621\t// ============================================================================\n   622\t\n   623\tvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n   624\t    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n   625\t\n   626\t    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n   627\t        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n   628\t            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n   629\t\n   630\t            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n   631\t                if (i == j) continue;\n   632\t\n   633\t                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n   634\t\n   635\t                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n   636\t                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n   637\t\n   638\t                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n   639\t                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n   640\t\n   641\t                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n   642\t                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n   643\t                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n   644\t                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n   645\t\n   646\t                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n   647\t                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n   648\t            }\n   649\t\n   650\t            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n   651\t            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n   652\t        }\n   653\t\n   654\t        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n   655\t            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n   656\t        }\n   657\t    }\n   658\t}\n   659\t\n   660\t// ============================================================================\n   661\t// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n   662\t// ============================================================================\n   663\t\n   664\tvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n   665\t    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n   666\t\n   667\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n   668\t        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n   669\t        int bestT = 0;\n   670\t\n   671\t        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n   672\t        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n   673\t            val_t sum = ZERO;\n   674\t\n   675\t            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n   676\t                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n   677\t                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n   678\t                sum += pS * pB;\n   679\t            }\n   680\t\n   681\t            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n   682\t                maxCorr = sum;\n   683\t                bestT = Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t Returns the number of such pairs.\nsize_t computeActiveMask(size_t idxI, const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                         std::vector<uint8_t>& mask) {\n    const size_t n = triangles.size();\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    size_t count = 0;\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n        const bool active = (j != idxI) && !isCulledPair(triI, triangles[j]);\n        mask[j] = static_cast<uint8_t>(active);\n        count += active;\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n\n// Number of rand() calls consumed by one (non-culled) computeKij invocation.\nconstexpr unsigned long long RANDS_PER_KIJ = 4ULL * NUM_RAYS;\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter).\n// Must only be called for pairs that are not culled (see isCulledPair).\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\"\"\"\nassert old in s\ns=s.replace(old,new)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncat >> roomsim.cpp <<'EOF'\n\n// ============================================================================\n// MPI Helpers\n// ============================================================================\n\nstatic int g_rank = 0;\nstatic int g_size = 1;\n\n// printf that only produces output on rank 0\n#define rprintf(...)                         \\\n    do {                                     \\\n        if (g_rank == 0) printf(__VA_ARGS__); }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Row Partitioning\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Partition the rows among ranks so that each rank gets (approximately) the\n// same number of active (non-culled) pairs, which is proportional to both the\n// form factor work and the number of random numbers consumed. Also computes\n// the random stream offset at which this rank's first row begins.\nvoid partitionRows(SimulationState& state) {\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const int P = g_size;\n\n    // Step 1: each rank counts the active pairs for a naive block of rows.\n    std::vector<int> naiveCounts(P), naiveDispls(P);\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        size_t b = (n * static_cast<size_t>(r)) / P;\n        size_t e = (n * static_cast<size_t>(r + 1)) / P;\n        naiveDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(b);\n        naiveCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(e - b);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> rowActive(n, 0);\n    {\n        std::vector<uint8_t> mask(n);\n        const size_t b = static_cast<size_t>(naiveDispls[g_rank]);\n        const size_t e = b + static_cast<size_t>(naiveCounts[g_rank]);\n        for (size_t i = b; i < e; ++i) {\n            rowActive[i] = static_cast<int>(computeActiveMask(i, state.triangles, mask));\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                   rowActive.data(), naiveCounts.data(), naiveDispls.data(), MPI_INT,\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Step 2: prefix sums of active pairs (identical on all ranks).\n    std::vector<unsigned long long> prefix(n + 1, 0);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) prefix[i + 1] = prefix[i] + static_cast<unsigned long long>(rowActive[i]);\n    const unsigned long long total = prefix[n];\n\n    // Step 3: choose contiguous row ranges with balanced active-pair counts.\n    state.rowCounts.assign(P, 0);\n    state.rowDispls.assign(P, 0);\n    size_t row = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < P; ++r) {\n        state.rowDispls[r] = static_cast<int>(row);\n        if (r == P - 1) {\n            state.rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(n - row);\n            row = n;\n            continue;\n        }\n        // Target cumulative work at the end of rank r\n        const unsigned long long target = (total * static_cast<unsigned long long>(r + 1)) / P;\n        size_t end = row;\n        // Advance while adding the next row keeps us at or below target, or\n        // while the row contributes nothing (so we never fall behind).\n        while (end < n && prefix[end + 1] <= target) ++end;\n        // Make sure ranks get at least one row when enough rows exist\n        if (end == row && row < n && (n - row) > static_cast<size_t>(P - r)) end = row + 1;\n        // Leave enough rows for the remaining ranks if possible\n        const size_t remainingRanks = static_cast<size_t>(P - r - 1);\n        if (n > remainingRanks && end > n - remainingRanks) end = n - remainingRanks;\n        if (end < row) end = row;\n        state.rowCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(end - row);\n        row = end;\n    }\n\n    state.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowDispls[g_rank]);\n    state.numLocalRows = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowCounts[g_rank]);\n    state.rowEnd = state.rowBegin + state.numLocalRows;\n    state.randOffset = prefix[state.rowBegin] * RANDS_PER_KIJ;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh (replicated, deterministic)\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    rprintf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    rprintf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Distribute matrix rows among ranks\n    partitionRows(state);\n    rprintf(\"Distributed %zu rows over %d MPI ranks\\n\", state.numTriangles, g_size);\n\n    // Initialize matrices (local row blocks for the N x N ones)\n    state.kij.assign(state.numLocalRows * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.assign(state.numLocalRows * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.assign(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.assign(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.assign(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    rprintf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    // Fast-forward the random stream to where the sequential program would be\n    // when starting this rank's first row.\n    const unsigned long long pulls = RandomGenerator::enginePullsPerRand();\n    rng.discard(state.randOffset * pulls);\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    std::vector<uint8_t> mask(n);\n\n    for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        computeActiveMask(i, state.triangles, mask);\n        val_t* kijRow = &state.kij[state.idx2dLocal(i, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (!mask[j]) continue; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        int* tauRow = &state.tau[state.idx2dLocal(i, 0)];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            tauRow[j] = computeTau(state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    rprintf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t n = state.numTriangles;\n    const val_t* areas = state.areas.data();\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        const int ti = static_cast<int>(t);\n        val_t* radBt = &state.radB[state.idxTN(t, 0)];\n\n        for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n            const int* tauRow = &state.tau[state.idx2dLocal(i, 0)];\n            const val_t* kijRow = &state.kij[state.idx2dLocal(i, 0)];\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n                if (i == j) continue;\n\n                int tauij = tauRow[j];\n\n                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n                if (ti < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = kijRow[j];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n            radBt[i] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n        }\n\n        // Share this timestep's radiosities with all ranks (N floats). Tau is\n        // >= 1 for all i != j, so no rank needs another rank's values from the\n        // current timestep before this exchange.\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                       radBt, state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT,\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n            rprintf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    rprintf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    const size_t T = state.numTimesteps;\n\n    // Contiguous copies of the time series (columns of radB) for cache locality\n    std::vector<val_t> pS(T), pB(T);\n    for (size_t tt = 0; tt < T; ++tt) pS[tt] = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, state.sourceIndex)];\n\n    for (size_t i = state.rowBegin; i < state.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        for (size_t tt = 0; tt < T; ++tt) pB[tt] = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < T; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < T; ++tt) {\n                sum += pS[tt - t] * pB[tt];\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n\n    // Gather all distances on every rank\n    MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL,\n                   state.distances.data(), state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDispls.data(), MPI_FLOAT,\n                   MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Collective: all ranks must call this; the result is identical on all ranks.\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    rprintf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Count non-zero local form factors and reduce (collective, done first so\n    // that early returns below cannot desynchronize the ranks).\n    long long localNonZeroKij = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.kij.size(); ++i) {\n        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) localNonZeroKij++;\n    }\n    long long nonZeroKij = 0;\n    MPI_Allreduce(&localNonZeroKij, &nonZeroKij, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_SUM, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n        if (d < 0) {\n            allNonNegative = false;\n            rprintf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n        }\n        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n            rprintf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n            return false;\n        }\n        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n        sumDist += d;\n        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n    }\n\n    rprintf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n    rprintf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n    rprintf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n        rprintf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n    }\n\n    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n                receivedEnergy++;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    rprintf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n\n    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n        rprintf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n    const size_t totalPairs = state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles;\n    rprintf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %lld/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n            nonZeroKij, totalPairs,\n            100.0f * static_cast<val_t>(nonZeroKij) / static_cast<val_t>(totalPairs));\n\n    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n        rprintf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!allNonNegative) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    rprintf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Hash for Verification\n// ============================================================================\n\nuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        uint32_t bits;\n        std::memcpy(&bits, &state.distances[i], sizeof(bits));\n        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(bits) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n// ============================================================================\n\nint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n    int subdivisions = 0;\n    int triangles = 20;\n    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n        subdivisions++;\n        triangles *= 4;\n    }\n    return subdivisions;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Main\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    rprintf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    rprintf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -o           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    rprintf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Barrier + wall clock, used to time phases consistently across ranks\nstatic double syncTime() {\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    return wallTime();\n}\n\nstatic long toMs(double seconds) {\n    return static_cast<long>(seconds * 1000.0 + 0.5);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n runSimulation(state);\n\n    double endSim = syncTime();\n    long simDuration = toMs(endSim - startSim);\n\n    rprintf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", simDuration);\n    rprintf(\"\\n\");\n\n    // Instead we\n// use the polynomial jump method (Haramoto, Matsumoto, Nishimura, Panneton,\n// L'Ecuyer 2008): with phi the characteristic polynomial of the state\n// transition A, the state after J steps is g(A) * S where g = x^J mod phi.\n// phi is recovered at run time with Berlekamp-Massey, so no large constant\n// tables are needed.\n\nnamespace mtjump {\n\nconstexpr size_t MT_N = 624;\nconstexpr size_t MT_M = 397;\nconstexpr uint32_t MT_MATRIX_A = 0x9908b0dfu;\nconstexpr uint32_t MT_UPPER = 0x80000000u;\nconstexpr uint32_t MT_LOWER = 0x7fffffffu;\nconstexpr size_t MT_DEG = 19937;                 // degree of the characteristic polynomial\nconstexpr size_t PW = (MT_DEG + 64) / 64;        // words for a polynomial of degree <= MT_DEG (312)\n\nusing Poly = std::vector<uint64_t>;              // bit i == coefficient of x^i\n\ninline uint32_t twist(uint32_t x0, uint32_t x1, uint32_t xm) {\n    uint32_t y = (x0 & MT_UPPER) | (x1 & MT_LOWER);\n    return xm ^ (y >> 1) ^ ((y & 1u) ? MT_MATRIX_A : 0u);\n}\n\ninline bool getBit(const Poly& p, size_t i) { return (p[i >> 6] >> (i & 63)) & 1u; }\ninline void setBit(Poly& p, size_t i) { p[i >> 6] |= uint64_t(1) << (i & 63); }\n\n// dst ^= src << k   (src has nsrc words; dst must have room)\ninline void shiftXor(Poly& dst, const Poly& src, size_t nsrc, size_t k) {\n    const size_t ws = k >> 6;\n    const unsigned bs = static_cast<unsigned>(k & 63);\n    if (bs == 0) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < nsrc; ++i) dst[i + ws] ^= src[i];\n    } else {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < nsrc; ++i) {\n            dst[i + ws] ^= src[i] << bs;\n            dst[i + ws + 1] ^= src[i] >> (64 - bs);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Reduce p (any size) modulo phi (degree MT_DEG); result has degree < MT_DEG.\ninline void reduceMod(Poly& p, const Poly& phi) {\n    for (size_t bit = p.size() * 64; bit-- > MT_DEG;) {\n        if (getBit(p, bit)) shiftXor(p, phi, PW, bit - MT_DEG);\n    }\n}\n\n// Spread the 32 bits of v into the even bit positions of a 64-bit word.\ninline uint64_t spread32(uint32_t v) {\n    uint64_t x = v;\n    x = (x | (x << 16)) & 0x0000FFFF0000FFFFULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 8))  & 0x00FF00FF00FF00FFULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 4))  & 0x0F0F0F0F0F0F0F0FULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 2))  & 0x3333333333333333ULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 1))  & 0x5555555555555555ULL;\n    return x;\n}\n\n// a^2 mod phi (squaring over GF(2) just spreads the bits)\ninline Poly squareMod(const Poly& a, const Poly& phi) {\n    Poly r(2 * PW + 1, 0);\n    for (size_t w = 0; w < PW; ++w) {\n        r[2 * w] = spread32(static_cast<uint32_t>(a[w]));\n        r[2 * w + 1] = spread32(static_cast<uint32_t>(a[w] >> 32));\n    }\n    reduceMod(r, phi);\n    r.resize(PW);\n    return r;\n}\n\n// a*x mod phi\ninline void mulXMod(Poly& a, const Poly& phi) {\n    Poly r(PW + 1, 0);\n    shiftXor(r, a, PW, 1);\n    reduceMod(r, phi);\n    r.resize(PW);\n    a.swap(r);\n}\n\n// x^J mod phi by square-and-multiply\ninline Poly powXMod(unsigned long long J, const Poly& phi) {\n    Poly r(PW, 0);\n    setBit(r, 0);  // r = 1\n    for (int bit = 63; bit >= 0; --bit) {\n        r = squareMod(r, phi);\n        if ((J >> bit) & 1ULL) mulXMod(r, phi);\n    }\n    return r;\n}\n\n// Characteristic polynomial of the MT19937 recurrence via Berlekamp-Massey on\n// the top bit of the raw recurrence (a non-degenerate linear functional of the\n// state; since the polynomial is primitive, the minimal polynomial of any such\n// sequence is the full characteristic polynomial).\ninline const Poly& characteristicPolynomial() {\n    static Poly phi;\n    if (!phi.empty()) return phi;\n\n    // Generate the bit sequence\n    const size_t nbits = 2 * MT_DEG + 128;\n    std::vector<uint32_t> x(MT_N);\n    {\n        std::mt19937 seeder(0x5EEDu);\n        for (auto& v : x) v = static_cast<uint32_t>(seeder());\n    }\n    size_t pos = 0;\n    const size_t nwordsSeq = nbits / 64 + 2;\n    Poly rev(nwordsSeq + 2, 0);  // reversed sequence: rev bit t == s[nbits-1-t]\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < nbits; ++k) {\n        uint32_t nx = twist(x[pos], x[(pos + 1) % MT_N], x[(pos + MT_M) % MT_N]);\n        x[pos] = nx;\n        pos = (pos + 1) % MT_N;\n        if (nx & MT_UPPER) setBit(rev, nbits - 1 - k);\n    }\n\n    // Berlekamp-Massey over GF(2) (bit-packed)\n    const size_t pwords = nbits / 64 + 2;\n    Poly C(pwords, 0), B(pwords, 0), T(pwords, 0);\n    setBit(C, 0);\n    setBit(B, 0);\n    size_t L = 0, m = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < nbits; ++i) {\n        // discrepancy d = sum_{j=0..L} C_j * s[i-j] = sum_j C_j * rev[(nbits-1-i)+j]\n        const size_t off = nbits - 1 - i;\n        const size_t ws = off >> 6;\n        const unsigned bs = static_cast<unsigned>(off & 63);\n        const size_t nw = (L >> 6) + 1;\n        uint64_t acc = 0;\n        for (size_t w = 0; w < nw; ++w) {\n            uint64_t win = rev[ws + w] >> bs;\n            if (bs) win |= rev[ws + w + 1] << (64 - bs);\n            acc ^= C[w] & win;\n        }\n        const bool d = __builtin_parityll(acc);\n        if (!d) {\n            ++m;\n            continue;\n        }\n        T = C;\n        shiftXor(C, B, pwords - (m >> 6) - 1, m);\n        if (2 * L <= i) {\n            L = i + 1 - L;\n            B.swap(T);\n            m = 1;\n        } else {\n            ++m;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (L != MT_DEG) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"MT jump: unexpected linear complexity %zu\\n\", L);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // phi(x) = x^L * C(1/x)  (reciprocal of the connection polynomial)\n    phi.assign(PW, 0);\n    for (size_t j = 0; i < MT_N; ++i) { ss >> v; mx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(v); }\n        ss >> v;\n        mp = static_cast<size_t>(v);\n    }\n\n    // Build the canonical window S = (x_q, ..., x_{q+623}) where x_{q+624} is the\n    // next raw value the engine will produce. };\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < MT_N; ++t) {\n            const size_t q = mp + t;  // S[t] = x_q\n            S[t] = q < MT_N ? mx[q] : twist(getX(q - MT_N), getX(q - MT_N + 1), getX(q - MT_N + MT_M));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // g = x^J mod phi\n    const Poly& phi = characteristicPolynomial();\n    Poly g = powXMod(J, phi);\n\n    // Horner: R = sum_k g_k A^k S, with R kept in a circular buffer.\n    std::vector<uint32_t> R(MT_N, 0);\n    size_t base = 0; t < MT_N; ++t) R[(base + t) % MT_N] ^= S[t];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Load back: canonical window with index 624 (block exhausted)\n    {\n        std::stringstream ss;\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < MT_N; ++t) ss << R[(base + t) % MT_N] << ' ';\n        ss << MT_N;\n        ss >> eng;\n    }\n}\n\n// Verify the jump implementation against discard() on this platform.\ninline void selfTest() {\n    static bool done = false;\n    if (done) return;\n    done = true;\n    const unsigned long long jumps[2] = {1000003ULL, 777ULL};\n    for (int c = 0; c < 2; ++c) {\n        std::mt19937 a(12345u), b(12345u);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 10 * c; ++i) { (void)a(); (void)b(); }  // second case: mid-block start\n        a.discard(jumps[c]);\n        jump(b, jumps[c]);\n        for (int i = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011Cfw5GVoa2iQbpL4YVqVi3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The roomsim benchmark is now MPI-parallel, producing bit-identical results to the sequential original at every rank count tested. Only `roomsim/roomsim.cpp` and `roomsim/CMakeLists.txt` were changed, the executable is still `roomsim`, and the program always runs under MPI.\n\n**How the work is distributed**\n\n- **Row-block decomposition.** The N×N form-factor and time-delay matrices are split into contiguous row blocks. Each rank stores and computes only its own rows, so per-rank memory for the big matrices drops by a factor of P. Rows are partitioned so every rank gets an equal share of non-culled triangle pairs, which is what drives both work and random-number use.\n- **Exact random stream reproduction.** The original draws all Monte Carlo samples from one `mt19937(42)` stream. Each rank computes how many samples the sequential code would have consumed before its first row and fast-forwards to that point. Short distances use `discard`. Long distances use a logarithmic-time Mersenne Twister jump: the characteristic polynomial is recovered at run time with Berlekamp–Massey, then x^J mod φ is applied with Horner's rule. The jump is self-verified against `discard` at startup and aborts on mismatch. A 1.7-billion-step jump takes about 60 ms instead of 550 ms, and the cost no longer grows with rank index.\n- **Wave propagation.** Each rank updates radiosity for its rows and the row of N floats is all-gathered once per timestep. Since every delay is at least one step, there is no intra-timestep dependency and semantics are preserved.\n- **Distances and validation.** Cross-correlation runs on local rows, results are all-gathered, and the non-zero form-factor count is reduced across ranks. Validation returns the same verdict on every rank so exit codes stay consistent. Only rank 0 prints.\n- **CMake** now uses `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`.\n\n**Verification**\n\nHashes from `-o` output match the sequential build for all configurations tried, including odd rank counts and more ranks than triangles (32 ranks on 20 triangles).\n\n| Config | Ranks tested | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n| 20, 80, 320 triangles, various `-t -s -r` | 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 29, 32 | identical hash, validation passes |\n| 1280 triangles | 3, 16, 29, 64 | identical hash |\n| 5120 triangles | 32, 64, 128, 256 | identical hash, validation passes |\n\n**Performance** (form-factor phase dominates; this host has 256 logical threads)\n\n| Problem | Sequential | MPI | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 320 tri | 1.48 s | 0.22 s at 7 ranks | 6.7× |\n| 1280 tri | 26.3 s | 0.70 s at 64 ranks | 38× |\n| 5120 tri | ~440 s est. | 15.8 s / 8.0 s / 4.5 s at 32 / 64 / 128 ranks | ~1.9× per doubling |\n\nPer-rank form-factor times stay within roughly 5–15% of each other, so the partition is well balanced. Going from 128 to 256 ranks gave little gain here, which is consistent with SMT contention on this machine rather than the decomposition.\n\nOne behavioral note: the progress line during form-factor computation now reports rank 0's local row progress, and a new line reports min/max per-rank form-factor time as a load-balance diagnostic."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1185,"cache_read_input_tokens":85057,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1185},"output_tokens":710,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6b7a688d-f3a3-4fe7-9fce-c0e80df4a2ba","uuid":"6651f686-a242-4d27-a56b-1422a823f9cf","timestamp":"2026-10-11T20:23:44.744Z","request_id":"req_011Cfw5GUauCt4VBczoNTLen"}
